\displaystyle=\frac{1}{I_{T}}\omega^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}+\frac{1}{I_{C}}\omega^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}+\frac{J_{T}}{I_{T}J_{C}J}\omega^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}+\frac{J_{T}}{I_{C}J_{C}J}\omega^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{I}\xi^{\textsf{B}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}+\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}J}\xi^{\textsf{S}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}-\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}}\frac{1}{IJ}\xi^{\textsf{BS}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}.

We can express the optimal adjustment
\bm{\beta}:=[\beta_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}\ \beta_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}]
as
\bm{\beta}=\bm{Z}^{-1}\bm{u}
, where
\bm{Z}
is a
2\times 2
block matrix with block entries

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}},{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{I_{T}}Z^{\textsf{B}}+\frac{J_{T}}{I_{T}J_{C}J}Z^{\textsf{BS}}

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{I}Z^{\textsf{B}}-\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}J}Z^{\textsf{S}}+\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}}\frac{1}{IJ}Z^{\textsf{BS}}

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{I_{C}}Z^{\textsf{B}}+\frac{J_{T}}{I_{C}J_{C}J}Z^{\textsf{BS}}.

and
\bm{u}
is given by

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{I_{T}}u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}-\frac{1}{I}(u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}-u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}})

\displaystyle+\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}J}(u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}-u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}})

\displaystyle+\frac{J_{T}}{I_{T}J_{C}J}u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}-\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}IJ}(u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}}-u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}})

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{I_{C}}u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-\frac{1}{I}(u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}})

\displaystyle+\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}J}(u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}})

\displaystyle+\frac{J_{T}}{I_{C}J_{C}J}u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-\frac{J_{T}}{J_{C}IJ}(u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,1,0}\operatorname{ib}})

F.4
Interacted Estimators for the Seller Spillover Effect

In this case, the variance is given by

\displaystyle\Var(\tau_{\text{ss}}(\beta))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{J_{T}}\omega^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}+\frac{1}{J_{C}}\omega^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}+\frac{I_{T}}{J_{T}I_{C}I}\omega^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}+\frac{I_{T}}{J_{C}I_{C}I}\omega^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}}\frac{1}{I}\xi^{\textsf{B}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}-\frac{1}{J}\xi^{\textsf{S}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}-\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}}\frac{1}{IJ}\xi^{\textsf{BS}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}},

and the adjustment has the components

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{J_{T}}Z^{\textsf{S}}+\frac{I_{T}}{J_{T}I_{C}I}Z^{\textsf{BS}}

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}I}Z^{\textsf{B}}+\frac{1}{J}Z^{\textsf{S}}+\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}}\frac{1}{IJ}Z^{\textsf{BS}}

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{J_{C}}Z^{\textsf{S}}+\frac{I_{T}}{J_{C}I_{C}I}Z^{\textsf{BS}}.

and
\bm{u}
is given by

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}I}(u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}-u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{J_{T}}u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}-\frac{1}{J}(u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}-u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}})

\displaystyle+\frac{I_{T}}{J_{T}I_{C}I}u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}-\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}IJ}(u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}-u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}})

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}I}(u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-u^{\textsf{B}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{J_{C}}u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-\frac{1}{J}(u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-u^{\textsf{S}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}})

\displaystyle+\frac{I_{T}}{J_{C}I_{C}I}u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-\frac{I_{T}}{I_{C}IJ}(u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\operatorname{cc}}-u^{\textsf{BS}}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\operatorname{is}}).
